Specifically, we show how ε-commutativity can be exploited for lifted model construction, downstream probabilistic inference, and prove strict bounds on the induced approximation error, thereby ensuring the practical applicability of lifted model construction while maintaining highly accurate query results. These theoretical guarantees are confirmed empirically, demonstrating comparable query accuracy at lower runtime.

</details>


### [78] [Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding: Solvability, Complexity, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24585)
*Andrea Di Nezza,Mihir Patel,Fabio Fagnani,Sara Bernardini*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了PS-MAPF问题，给出了完全的可解性刻画，证明了优化目标的NP-hard性，并设计了三种算法，其中PPP算法在基准测试中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化高密度存储系统需要多智能体在稀缺的关键资源间移动并最终停泊，现有MAPF变体未充分考虑此类场景中的枢轴访问和匿名停泊约束，缺乏系统的可解性分析和高效求解方法。

Method: 首先对PS-MAPF进行形式化定义，并利用图论给出基于有效距离的可解性充要条件；随后通过归约证明最小化makespan和flowtime的NP-hard性；最后提出完整基线、基于SAT的最优求解器以及启发式算法PPP。

Result: 在2-边连通图上所有实例可解；任意连通图上的可解性由有效距离与未占用顶点数关系决定。优化目标在单枢轴时即NP-hard。PPP在基准上解决74-89%的实例，makespan和flowtime比基线低一个数量级。

Conclusion: PS-MAPF具有清晰的结构特性，虽然最优求解困难，但所提PPP算法在实用中高效且接近最优，为自动化存储系统的路径规划提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Automated high-density storage systems (warehouses, robotic parking, plant logistics, etc.) require fleets of agents to move through scarce task-critical resources and then park without obstructing future operations. We introduce Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding (PS-MAPF), a MAPF variant in which a subset of tasked agents must each visit one of a set of interchangeable pivots (e.g., workstations) before the entire fleet terminates at anonymous stations, one agent per station. We characterize solvability completely: every instance on a 2-edge-connected graph is solvable, and, on arbitrary connected graphs, a structural effective-distance measure relative to the number of unoccupied vertices gives a necessary and sufficient condition. We prove that minimizing station-makespan or station-flowtime is NP-hard already with a single pivot. We present three algorithms, a complete baseline, a SAT-based optimal solver, and Pivot-Prioritized Planning (PPP), the last solving 74-89% of benchmark instances with makespan and flowtime orders of magnitude below the baseline.

</details>


### [79] [Causal Modelling of Support Interventions for Student Competency Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24632)
*Francesca Mangili,Alessandro Antonucci,Rafael Cabañas*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出采用结构因果建模替代传统概率模型进行教育评估，支持干预和反事实推理，并通过专家提供逻辑性结构方程构建协议，以算法技能评估数据为例展示其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心理测量模型（如项目反应理论）仅基于概率关联进行能力推断，无法支持干预（如提示）和反事实情景分析，限制了教育诊断和个性化干预的精确性。

Method: 提出结构因果建模协议，要求专家仅提供逻辑性结构方程（非概率假设），通过显式建模干预与反事实推理，扩展传统关联模型的推理能力。

Result: 在评估义务教育阶段学生算法技能的复杂任务数据上，成功展示了干预建模和反事实分析的实际可操作性，且无需依赖较难成立的概率假设。

Conclusion: 结构因果建模为教育评估提供了更丰富的推理框架，可支持更精准的干预设计与教学策略优化，尽管依赖专家知识，但所需信息逻辑性强且假设更稳健。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of student competencies is essential for enabling educators to identify individual needs, design targeted interventions, and evaluate the effectiveness of educational strategies. Empirical assessment procedures are typically grounded in psychometric models, such as item response theory, which relate student competence levels to performance on assessment tasks. In this paper, we advocate adopting a structural causal modelling approach to educational assessment, moving beyond probabilistic belief updating toward a framework that explicitly supports interventional and counterfactual reasoning. We propose a corresponding protocol for its construction and analyse the practical relevance of forms of reasoning that remain inaccessible to standard associative models, including the explicit modelling of interventions such as hints and the related counterfactual scenario analysis. Although our protocol requires the structural equations to be elicited from experts, the necessary information is purely logical and does not rely on probabilistic, less tenable assumptions. We illustrate the approach using data from an assessment that employs complex tasks designed to measure compulsory school student algorithmic skills.

</details>


### [80] [Parason: Revealing Subtask and Trial Parallelism in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24658)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Zijian Zhang,Jiaxuan Gao,Shusheng Xu,Yi Wu,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Parason框架，通过并行推理轨迹学习和PA-GRPO优化，实现约1.7倍加速且保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归解码在困难任务中产生严重延迟，而现有并行方法仅关注子任务并行，忽视了试验并行这一主要可并行计算形式。

Method: 识别并利用子任务并行与试验并行，将顺序推理转换为上下文无关语法定义的并行轨迹，并用并行感知的PA-GRPO联合优化准确率、延迟和并行度，推理时通过工具调用执行并行结构。

Result: 在AIME24和AIME25等数学基准上平均加速约1.7倍，同时保持竞争力准确率。

Conclusion: 试验并行是并行推理计算的主体，Parason通过结构化并行轨迹和强化学习有效平衡准确率与延迟，为提升LLM推理效率提供了新途径。

Abstract: Scaling test-time reasoning has substantially improved the problem-solving ability of large language models (LLMs), but standard autoregressive decoding still executes long reasoning traces sequentially, creating severe latency for difficult tasks (up to days and weeks). Parallel reasoning offers a natural remedy. However, prior systems primarily focus on Subtask Parallelism, where the model learns to decompose a high-level task into smaller chunks that can be solved independently. This approach overlooks another pervasive form of parallelism: Trial Parallelism, where multiple speculative attempts explore, verify, and aggregate competing hypotheses in parallel. In this paper, we introduce Parason, which reveals and learns both forms of parallelism in LLM reasoning. Our analysis identifies Trial Parallelism as the majority of parallelizable reasoning computation (65.5% in DeepSeek-V4's reasoning steps in HLE), and it becomes increasingly dominant on hard problems. Guided by this taxonomy, Parason converts sequential reasoning traces into structured parallel trajectories with a context-free grammar, then trains models with Parallelism-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (PA-GRPO), whose reward jointly balances accuracy, latency, and the two parallelism ratios. At inference time, Parason executes the learned parallel structure through tool calls, translating theoretical savings to real-world wall-clock acceleration. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks including AIME24 and AIME25 show that Parason achieves an average acceleration about 1.7$\times$ while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [Evidence Blindness in Direct Corpus Interaction: Persistent Navigation with AtlasNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24764)
*Hongyu Guo,Zhiyu Zheng,Zhao Cao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AtlasNav框架，通过一次性构建持久化多视角语料库导航结构，解决大语言模型智能体在有限交互预算下因证据渐进性丢失导致的Evidence Blindness问题，在BrowseComp-Plus上达到92.05%准确率并降低30.21%在线推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接语料交互模式下，有限交互预算导致证据可能无法被检索、文档未被打开或关键片段未被发现，形成渐进性证据丢失（Evidence Blindness）。现有动态工作区方法每次查询都重建交互空间，缺乏可复用的语料库组织，导致结构恢复主要在线进行，效率低下。

Method: 提出AtlasNav，将大规模智能体搜索形式化为有限预算下对可复用语料库结构的导航。该方法将语料库离线组织为多视角的Corpus Atlas，每个查询只需在此持久化结构上自适应导航，无需重复重建共享结构。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，AtlasNav取得92.05%的严格准确率，相比动态工作区SOTA降低30.21%的在线推理成本；在匹配预算下更早实现完整证据，更快逼近证据供给的经验参考；在PhantomWiki的不同语料组织和10K-1M规模控制下同样有效，并成功迁移到异构企业知识场景。

Conclusion: 智能体搜索的性能不仅取决于证据的可访问性，更取决于语料库的表示方式——将语料组织为可复用结构能使有限交互变为高效导航，这为大规模智能体搜索提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond conventional retrieval-augmented generation toward direct interaction with external corpora. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) keeps the full corpus accessible, yet reachable evidence can remain unusable under finite interaction budgets. Required evidence may fail to surface, a surfaced supporting document may remain unopened, or an opened document may fail to expose its decisive fragment. We call this progressive silent loss Evidence Blindness and quantify it through stage-wise evidence realization. Within the DCI paradigm, raw interaction adds little reusable corpus organization, while dynamic-workspace methods reconstruct a query-conditioned interaction space from each query and trajectory. In both cases, useful structure is recovered largely online. We instead formulate large-scale agentic search as finite-budget navigation over reusable corpus structure. We introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that retains direct corpus interaction but organizes the corpus once into a Corpus Atlas, allowing each query to navigate adaptively rather than reconstruct shared structure. On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav achieves 92.05% strict accuracy while reducing recorded online inference cost by 30.21% relative to the prior dynamic-workspace state of the art. Under matched budgets, it realizes the complete required evidence earlier and approaches the same model's evidence-supplied empirical reference more rapidly. The same representation principle remains effective under PhantomWiki's distinct corpus organization and controlled 10K-1M scaling, and transfers competitively to heterogeneous enterprise knowledge. These results show that agentic search depends not only on accessible evidence, but also on how the corpus is represented so that limited interaction becomes effective navigation.

</details>


### [82] [Right Diagnoses, Decorative Reasoning:A Perturbation Audit of Medical Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24790)
*Mengzhu Xu,Jifan Gao,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Xi Long*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过30种医学动机扰动算子对14个LLM在4个医学QA基准上的审计，发现CoT与答案解耦率高达72.9%，链的扰动不影响准确性，移除CoT也不降低性能，表明医学CoT更多是文档而非推理证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT忠实性探测忽略医学成本，医学LLM评估将链视为黑箱；临床医生依赖CoT作为推理证据但缺乏系统验证。

Method: 设计30种医学动机算子（严重性反转、否定翻转、人口统计学交换、证据消融）编辑链和问题，结合链更新与答案翻转的联合分析，对14个LLM在4个基准上测试，并由两名认证临床医生重注释197个扰动问题。

Result: 破坏性编辑下CDR为72.9%，链损坏不影响准确率，移除CoT不降低准确率；98.5%扰动问题金标注仍合理；模式跨微调类型和规模一致，闭源模型答案侧信号一致。

Conclusion: 医学CoT普遍不忠实，更像事后文档而非推理依据；CDR框架可作为审计医学CoT忠实性的可复用标尺。

Abstract: Clinicians read chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales as evidence of medical reasoning, but whether the visible chain plays that role is rarely tested. General-domain CoT-faithfulness probes ignore clinical cost, and medical LLM evaluations treat the chain as a black box. We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [83] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种严格因果的流式视频异常检测器，基于对角线性状态空间递归，固定状态O(1)更新，无剪辑缓冲，在Apple M3 Pro上实测每帧0.74-0.77ms，但精度低于非因果SSM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mamba式SSM方法仍依赖内部剪辑/窗口缓冲，缺乏时域记忆与检测延迟的理论联系，且仅在GPU上评测吞吐，未针对实际边缘硬件。

Method: 采用冻结视觉骨干加因果下一嵌入预测训练，核心为带输入和状态相关衰减门的对角线性状态空间递归，推导衰减谱与检测延迟及可捕获最短异常的闭式关系。

Result: 在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上帧级AUC为67.9%和70.2%，实测延迟1.6和18.4帧，远低于基于学习基衰减预测的57-59帧稳定界；端到端每帧0.74-0.77ms（超1300 FPS）。

Conclusion: 事件边界门而非基衰减主导响应性，门贡献依赖数据集规模，小数据集上损害精度；立即下一步是缩小精度差距并扩展至更大基准。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [84] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CES-PK问题，用轻量级符号约束验证LLM生成的KGQA候选答案，采用三值语义处理知识图谱不完整性，在Hetionet上提升精度且保持召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在KGQA中可能错误接地；语义解析方法脆弱，纯推理方法缺乏形式保证，需要一种兼顾鲁棒性与可验证性的互补方案。

Method: 定义CES-PK，仅用问题导出的类型/关系/排除约束筛选候选，采用satisfied/violated/unknown三值语义适应开放世界假设，不构造完整逻辑形式。

Result: 过滤违反约束的候选提升精度，保留未明确违反者维持召回；满足约束作为正符号证据用于排序，在Hetionet上验证有效。

Conclusion: 轻量符号约束+三值语义是LLM-KGQA答案验证的有效途径，兼顾精度与召回，适应不完整知识图谱。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [85] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FedV-KGQA框架，在垂直划分的知识图谱上实现多跳问答，无需跨组织共享原始数据或运行时通信，性能接近集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中知识图谱问答数据因治理和主权限制分布在不同组织，集中式系统无法处理事实分散在垂直分区孤岛中的多跳问题。

Method: 结合本地图增强与知识图谱嵌入，确保原始三元组和关系参数不出本地；通过主题实体锚定机制将问题定位到正确图邻域，避免运行时跨孤岛通信。

Result: 在三个基准上评估12种模型配置，性能强且接近集中式，可泛化到3跳推理，对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA有效解决垂直分区知识图谱的多跳问答，兼顾隐私与性能，为联邦场景下的知识推理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [86] [Recursive Experiential-Working Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24876)
*Zhaochen Yu,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Kaiyuan Chen,Zhe Zhao,Mengdi Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Ling Yang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Recuris递归经验-工作记忆架构，通过工作记忆跟踪任务进度并指导技能选择，将执行转化为证据以定位失败，并利用固定元代理进行验证门控的递归记忆更新，在四个长时程基准和十个模型上显著提升任务成功率，优势随交互长度增加而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中递归自我改进困难，因为历史记录增长会掩盖任务状态并导致技能调用错位，现有方法难以持续将经验转化为有效行为。

Method: 设计递归经验-工作记忆架构：工作记忆跟踪进度并选择技能，执行过程作为结构化证据定位失败，固定Meta-Agent将这些证据转化为局部化、验证门控的技能记忆更新，形成有界的递归记忆演化循环。

Result: 在37个模型-基准配对中35个提升成功率，将前沿模型推向SOTA：tau-bench上GPT-5.6 Sol提升+17.8，Claude Opus 5提升+15.6达到87.9%，SkillFlow上Qwen3.6-27B/35B分别提升+16.6/+13.5；最长任务上提升+32.2，常见长时程失败降低达80%。

Conclusion: 递归演化记忆可作为RSI的可扩展基础，使智能体持续将积累经验转化为更有效的长时程行为。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) remains hard in long-horizon tasks, where growing histories obscure the task state and misalign skill invocation. We introduce Recuris, a recursive Experiential-Working Memory architecture for long-horizon agent harnesses, in which Working Memory tracks task progress and guides skill selection from Experiential Memory, grounding skill use in current needs rather than the full history. This coupling also turns execution into structured evidence that localizes failures to specific memory components. Across tasks, a fixed Meta-Agent turns that evidence into localized, validation-gated updates to Skill Memory that reshape execution and yield new evidence, forming a bounded recursive memory-evolution loop. Across four long-horizon benchmarks and ten models, Recuris improves task success in 35 of the 37 completed model-benchmark pairs, carrying frontier models to SOTA-level task success: on tau-bench it adds +17.8 points to GPT-5.6 Sol and +15.6 to Claude Opus 5, taking Opus 5 to 87.9%, and +16.6/+13.5 points on Qwen3.6-27B/35B on SkillFlow. The advantage widens as the interaction horizon grows, to +32.2 points on the longest tasks, and common long-horizon failures fall by up to 80%. These results position recursively evolving memory as a scalable foundation for RSI, enabling agents to continuously transform accumulated experience into increasingly effective long-horizon behavior. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Recuris

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Effective Pivot Attack Detection via System and Network Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23731)
*Ava Powelson,Carson Kuzniar,Hyojoon Kim,Israat Haque*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: Stitch是一个基于主机的系统，利用可编程内核实时检测网络中的横向移动（pivoting）攻击，通过结合系统与网络层面信息，显著提升检测精度并降低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于边界的安全设备（如防火墙、入侵检测系统）无法有效防御利用失陷主机进行流量转发（pivoting）的现代攻击，因为这种中继流量看似合法，难以检测。现有防御方案存在处理延迟高、准确性低或依赖全网协作等缺陷，因此需要一种精准、轻量且独立的主机级检测方案。

Method: Stitch部署在主机上，利用可编程内核实时观察流经主机的网络流。它采用进程追踪技术，将系统级信息（如进程行为）与网络级信息（流入/流出连接）有效关联，通过匹配进出通信并识别其中的pivoting特征来判断是否发生攻击。

Result: 在两次独立的真实部署中，Stitch相比最先进的pivoting检测方案准确率提升31%，最大误报率仅为0.006%。

Conclusion: Stitch弥补了现有pivoting检测方案的不足，为网络中的脆弱主机提供了准确、轻量且独立的防护覆盖，能有效应对利用失陷主机作为跳板的现代攻击。

Abstract: Perimeter-based security appliances, such as firewalls or Intrusion Detection Systems, are ineffective against modern attacks that use pivoting, wherein attackers "pivot" traffic through compromised hosts to gain access to additional targets that would otherwise be inaccessible. Due to the legitimate appearance of the relayed traffic, pivoting is extremely difficult to detect. Although the consequences of these attacks are known to be severe, existing defenses suffer from drawbacks such as high processing delays, low accuracy, or reliance on network-wide participation, making them inconvenient or even ineffective. This work presents Stitch, a host-based system that uses the programmable kernel to detect pivoting in real time. By observing host-traversing flows, Stitch uses process tracing to effectively combine system and network-level information, connecting incoming and outgoing communications and identifying pivoting characteristics between them. Showing 31% gains in accuracy over state-of-the-art pivoting defenses and a maximum false positive rate of 0.006% over two separate real-world deployments, Stitch covers the gap in current pivot detection solutions by providing accurate, lightweight, and independent coverage for vulnerable hosts in a network.

</details>


### [88] [TrustShiftProbe: Characterizing, Benchmarking, and Defending Staged Trust Attacks on MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23763)
*Mehrdad Rostamzadeh,Sidhant Narula,Mohammad Ghasemigol,Daniel Takabi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出TrustShift攻击：恶意MCP服务器先在初始阶段表现正常以建立信任，达到触发阈值后才切换至恶意负载，绕过静态分析和运行时过滤；并构建TrustShiftProbe框架及SHIELD防御，将攻击成功率从69.5%降至42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP作为LLM代理连接外部工具的标准层，其开放性引入新的服务器端威胁，现有研究忽略服务器端临时性背叛行为，且该行为无法通过预部署静态分析检测。

Method: 提出TrustShiftProbe框架，包含状态化时间威胁模型、语言无关攻击引擎、SHIELD多层运行时防御（在MCP传输边界基于信任窗口行为基线审计负载）及九种攻击变体分类法。

Result: 在前沿专有及开源模型上，TrustShift攻击平均成功率69.5%，SHIELD将其降低至42.7%。

Conclusion: TrustShift暴露MCP服务器端安全盲区，SHIELD可有效缓解，但防御后仍有较高成功率，需进一步强化。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has emerged as the standard layer connecting Large Language Model agents to external tool backends. This openness introduces a severe server-side threat we term TrustShift: a compromised MCP server behaves benignly during an initial conditioning phase, building operational reliance and suppressing agent skepticism, before switching to an adversarial payload once an interaction threshold is reached. The evasion is temporal, not syntactic: benign at deploy time, the server's defection is invisible to predeployment static analysis, which sees only the honest phase. Switched payloads range from overt structural violations to schema-valid manipulations, the latter preserving outer protocol compliance to evade runtime middleware filters. Crucially, TrustShift originates in the server-controlled tool channel, not user prompts (unlike indirect prompt injection) or the transport (unlike man-in-the-middle): the adversary is the trusted server endpoint itself. We introduce TrustShiftProbe, an evaluation and defense framework with four contributions: (1) a stateful temporal threat model of the agent-server lifecycle as a benign conditioning phase followed by an adversarial defection at a trust horizon; (2) a language-agnostic attack engine that instantiates each variant as a compromised MCP server across four production domains; (3) SHIELD, a multi-tier, zero-oracle runtime defense at the MCP transport boundary that audits server payloads against behavioral baselines learned during clean trust windows; and (4) a taxonomy of nine TrustShift variants spanning three execution mechanisms (structural violation, semantic corruption, scope expansion) and three adversarial objectives (disruption, exfiltration, and their combination). Across frontier proprietary and open-weight models, TrustShift attacks achieve a 69.5% mean attack success rate that SHIELD mitigates to 42.7%.

</details>


### [89] [ROBBIN: Rowhammer-Based Backdoor Injection during Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23774)
*Saion K. Roy,Yufei Wang,A. Adam Ding,Yunsi Fei*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出硬件感知的Rowhammer后门攻击ROBBIN，通过DRAM位翻转模式指导权重页映射选择，提升跨设备攻击鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Rowhammer后门攻击忽略硬件实际位翻转特性，导致攻击成功率与正常精度受意外位翻转影响，跨设备性能不稳定。

Method: 先表征目标DRAM的位翻转模式，再迭代选择权重页映射以最大化攻击成功率并保持测试精度，将每次锤击位翻转纳入设计而非作为副作用。

Result: 在ResNet-20/VGG-16+CIFAR-10及三款DDR4芯片上，攻击成功率接近90%，测试精度保持83%以上，跨设备表现一致。

Conclusion: 硬件感知的位翻转整合能显著提升Rowhammer后门攻击的可靠性与跨设备泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing Rowhammer-based inference-time backdoor attacks design their bit-flip strategies purely at the algorithmic level, without accounting for the bit-flips that the underlying DRAM hardware will actually produce. This disconnection between the algorithmic backdoor construction and its hardware realization leads to unreliable attack performance, as collateral bit-flips at unintended locations degrade both the attack success rate (ASR) on triggered inputs and the test accuracy (TA) for normal inputs. Consequently, the performance of such attacks varies significantly across different DRAM devices, as each device presents a unique set of exploitable bit-flip locations. This work presents ROBBIN, a hardware-aware Rowhammer-based backdoor injection attack that integrates the device-specific vulnerability into the backdoor construction process. ROBBIN first characterizes the bit-flip patterns of a target DRAM and uses this information to iteratively select DRAM \textit{page} mappings for the model weights that would maximize ASR while preserving TA under Rowhammering. By treating every hammering-induced bit-flip as an integral part of the attack design rather than first constructing a hardware-agnostic backdoor and dismissing collateral flips as side effects, ROBBIN produces backdoors that remain robust across devices. Evaluated on ResNet-20 and VGG-16 with CIFAR-10 across three commodity DDR4 chips, ROBBIN consistently achieves close to 90\% ASR while maintaining TA above 83\%, demonstrating reliable backdoor efficacy across diverse DRAM devices.

</details>


### [90] [Tight Majorizations and Convergence Rates of Nuclear Norm Minimization IRLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23765)
*Christian Kümmerle,Tomas Masak,Dominik Stöger*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文严格分析了迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）方法在核范数最小化中的收敛速率，证明了调和均值权重算子的全局二次上界性质及其在功率均值族中的最优性，并在Schatten-1零空间性质下建立了全局线性收敛；对调和均值权重进一步证明了与维度无关的局部线性收敛速率，同时通过反例说明单侧权重无法达到该速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRLS是核范数最小化的自然方法，但其收敛速率和权重算子的作用缺乏理论理解，且文献中常用单侧权重（仅利用行或列空间信息）的收敛性能尚不明确，因此需要系统刻画权重选择对收敛速率的影响。

Method: 提出新的平滑核范数主导分析，证明调和均值权重定义全局二次上界；在功率均值权重族中证明调和均值的最优性；结合Schatten-1零空间性质分析不同权重下IRLS的全局线性收敛，并通过构造反例对比单侧与双侧权重的局部收敛极限。

Result: 建立IRLS在约束核范数最小化中的尖锐全局线性收敛速率；对调和均值权重获得与维度无关的局部线性收敛速率；证明单侧权重无法达到该速率，数值实验验证了理论并展示调和均值在方形、矩形及对抗初始化问题上的实际优势。

Conclusion: 调和均值权重是IRLS在核范数最小化中的最优选择之一，其双侧信息融合带来更优的收敛保证，为实际算法设计提供了理论指导，并揭示了单侧重加权方案的固有局限。

Abstract: Iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS) methods constitute a natural approach to nuclear norm minimization, but their convergence rates and the role of the weight operator have remained poorly understood. This paper establishes sharp convergence rates for IRLS methods for constrained nuclear norm minimization in low-rank recovery. A central ingredient is a new majorization analysis for the smoothed nuclear norm: we prove that the harmonic-mean weight operator defines a valid global quadratic majorizer. Furthermore, we show that this weight operator is optimal within the family of power-mean weights, clarifying why it improves over classical one-sided reweighting schemes that use only row- or column-space information. Under a Schatten-1 null space property, we prove global linear convergence of IRLS algorithms using a variety of weight operators, including the harmonic-mean weights. For IRLS with harmonic-mean weights, we prove a dimension-independent, locally linear convergence rate. We provide a counterexample showing that this dimension-independent local rate cannot in general be obtained for IRLS algorithms using one-sided weight operators, which predominate in the literature. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results and illustrate the practical advantage of harmonic-mean reweighting across square, rectangular, and adversarially initialized recovery problems.

</details>


### [91] [A Scenario-Based Evaluation of CRQC+AI Vulnerability Spectrum for TLS 1.3 Cryptographic Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23785)
*Noel Grover,Mussie Haile,Brad Pedersen,Eric Uner,Bradley J Erickson*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的情景估算模型，评估量子与AI加速对TLS 1.3密码依赖的风险，按证据层级区分机制支撑、偶然性支撑和假设性风险，并给出参数化场景、敏感性分析及显式修订协议。截至2026年中，未发现NIST算法被攻破，但RSA风险在2030-2032年超50%，PQC在2032-2035年后在特定偶然场景下转为非零风险。结论强调按2030/2031联邦截止日期强制迁移PQC，并补充密码敏捷性与混合加密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子风险研究多集中于资源估算或专家调查，缺乏可复现、可更新的情景推演工具，且对后量子密码（PQC）的潜在风险层级区分不足。作者旨在提供一个明确参数、可迭代修订的模型，以支持迁移时间表的压力测试，并弥补对哈希与对称原语风险的系统性评估缺口。

Method: 采用证据分层模型（机制支撑、偶然支撑、假设性风险），构建四场景能力模型，每个参数命名且可调整，提供伪代码与参数敏感性分析。通过显式可证伪分析，设定协议将观测数据与模型曲线比对，以驱动参数修订，使模型随历史数据积累而演化。

Result: 模型未显示任何NIST批准算法被攻破。RSA风险阈值跨50%的时间为2030-2032年；ML-KEM、ML-DSA、SLH-DSA及AES-256无已知突破。PQC（如格基方案）在2032-2035年后，仅在维度塌缩假设下出现非零风险。敏感性分析显示迁移时间表对不同参数假设的响应。

Conclusion: 建议按2030/2031联邦法定截止日强制迁移至PQC，并依据Mosca HNDL论证；同时强调密码敏捷性和混合加密作为必要补充，以应对不确定的威胁演进。模型本身作为方法论工具，支持持续更新与社区复核。

Abstract: This paper evaluates quantum and AI-accelerated risks to TLS 1.3 cryptographic dependencies under an evidence-tiered model, distinguishing mechanism-backed threats (Shor algorithm against RSA and ECC) from contingency-backed risks to lattice-based post-quantum cryptography (PQC) and hypothesis-only risks to hash-based and symmetric primitives. We do not identify any known breaks of ML-KEM, ML-DSA, SLH-DSA, or AES-256. Instead, we use explicit scenario assumptions, organized as a four-scenario capability model with parameters and pseudocode for reproducibility, to stress-test migration timelines accompanied by parameter sensitivity analysis and explicit falsification analysis. The primary methodological contribution is a reproducible scenario-estimation instrument together with its explicit update mechanics: every parameter is a named, anchored quantity that can be varied and the model rerun; a stated protocol maps observed conformance to, or deviation from, the modeled curves onto revisions of specific parameters, so progressive refinements can be tested against accumulating historical data. The paper is a methodological companion to quantum resource-estimation studies and to expert-elicitation timeline surveys such as the Global Risk Institute quantum threat reports, with its revision rules stated explicitly. As of mid-2026, the model does not show any NIST-approved algorithms as broken. Instead, the vulnerability spectrum under different scenarios shows RSA risk crossing the 50% threshold between 2030-2032 and the PQC risk becoming a non-zero risk after 2032-2035 under contingency scenarios conditional on the unproven dimension-collapse. We urge PQC migration as mandatory per the 2030 and 2031 federal deadlines and by Mosca HNDL reasoning, and that crypto-agility and hybrid cryptographic deployment be considered necessary complements to any PQC migration efforts.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond the Mandate: A Systematic Security Analysis of the Agent Payments Protocol (AP2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23858)
*Avital Aviv,Parth A. Gandh,Ron Bitton,Asaf Shabtai*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 对Google AP2 v0.2协议进行了系统性安全分析，识别出48种威胁和8个高风险漏洞，证明仅凭有效签名无法保证交易符合用户意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AP2协议保护签名后的交易数据，但授权前的代理交互和外部输入（如A2A消息和MCP调用）不受保护，且v0.2新增功能和部署假设需要重新评估。

Method: 基于角色、交易生命周期、部署架构和信任边界，将生命周期分为五个阶段，识别五种部署架构，使用MAESTRO建模威胁，用AIVSS评分，构建覆盖所有架构的测试平台并开发扫描器。

Result: 发现48种威胁、五种攻击家族，八种威胁在至少一种架构中达到高风险等级，通过五个概念验证演示了全部高风险威胁及缓解措施。

Conclusion: AP2 v0.2无法防止授权前上下文被操纵导致的交易意图篡改，有效签名不足以保证交易完整性，需要部署感知的检测与防护机制。

Abstract: The Agent Payments Protocol (AP2), introduced by Google, enables large language model (LLM)-driven shopping agents to authorize and execute payments on behalf of users. Its signed Checkout and Payment Mandates protect the integrity of transaction data after signing. Agent interactions and external inputs that shape a transaction before authorization remain outside that protection, including Agent-to-Agent Protocol (A2A) messages and Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls. Prior work identified replay and prompt-injection attacks in AP2 v0.1. AP2 v0.2 addresses some of these issues but adds capabilities and deployment assumptions that require renewed analysis. We present a systematic security analysis of AP2 v0.2 based on its roles, transaction lifecycle, deployment architectures, and trust boundaries. We divide the lifecycle into five phases and identify five deployment architectures. Using MAESTRO (Multi-Agent Environment, Security, Threat, Risk, Outcome), we model four threat actors, eleven attack surfaces, eighteen adversary capabilities, and six attacker goals. The resulting catalog contains 48 threats spanning five attack families. We score these threats with the Artificial Intelligence Vulnerability Scoring System (AIVSS), identifying eight that reach the High band in at least one architecture. Because no complete public AP2 deployment was available, we build a testbed spanning all five architectures and develop five proof-of-concept demonstrations covering all eight High-risk threats and their mitigations. We also develop a deployment-aware scanner that maps applicable threats to static, cross-role consistency, and adversarial checks. Our analysis shows that valid mandate signatures alone do not ensure that an agent-mediated transaction reflects the user's intent when its pre-authorization context is manipulated.

</details>


### [93] [GAP-Prompt: Gated Adaptive Prompting for Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23782)
*Trung-Anh Dang,Duy-Cuong Bui,Ngoc-Son Vu,Christel Vrain,Vincent Nguyen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP-Prompt方法，通过实例级自适应提示机制解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的持续学习方法多采用静态任务级提示策略，忽视了任务内部细粒度的样本差异，导致模型难以适应多样化的实例特征。

Method: GAP-Prompt包含三个协同模块：实例条件门控动态选择提示注入层，动态知识融合实现当前与历史提示的实例感知聚合，共享提示蒸馏在早期层锚定基础知识以缓解遗忘。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R和CUB-200上均取得最优结果，其中CUB-200上准确率达87.29%，逼近联合训练上限88.00%，显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 实例级自适应提示机制能有效提升持续学习性能，尤其对细粒度任务效果显著，为提示学习方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Continual learning faces the persistent challenge of catastrophic forgetting, where sequential task updates degrade previously acquired knowledge. While prompt-based methods integrated with pre-trained models offer a compelling solution by freezing the backbone, they often rely on static, task-level prompting strategies that overlook fine-grained intra-task diversity. In this paper, we propose Gated Adaptive Prompting (GAP-Prompt), a novel method that introduces instance-level adaptability to the prompting process. GAP-Prompt consists of three synergistic modules: (1) instance-conditioned gating, which dynamically determines optimal prompt injection layers for each individual image; (2) dynamic knowledge fusion, which performs instance-aware aggregation of current and historical prompts, enabling knowledge integration across tasks; and (3) shared prompt distillation, which anchors foundational knowledge in early shared layers to mitigate forgetting. Extensive evaluations on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-R, and CUB-200 benchmarks demonstrate that GAP-Prompt consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, on the fine-grained CUB-200 dataset, GAP-Prompt reaches 87.29% accuracy, approaching the joint training upper bound (88.00%) and outperforming existing methods by a significant margin.

</details>


### [94] [STAIN-FL: Stealthy Targeted Attack Injection with Contextual Triggers in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23952)
*Ashlinder Kaur,Purnima Murali Mohan,Zengxiang Li,Tram Truong-Huu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出STAIN-FL，一种利用自然监控条件作为触发器、结合标签操纵与梯度掩蔽的隐蔽后门攻击框架，在非IID联邦视频异常检测中实现低检测率、高持续性攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦视频异常检测中服务器难以验证客户端数据，恶意客户端可通过更新注入后门，现有攻击缺乏隐蔽性和现实场景适配性。

Method: 以低光、室内、人群密度等自然场景条件为上下文触发器，组合异常到正常的标签操纵与最小更新坐标的梯度掩蔽，保持干净精度同时触发误分类。

Result: 在UCF-Crime数据集上，稀疏攻击下干净精度下降低于2%，峰值后门准确率在FedAvg为56.7%、FedProx为54.2%，且FedAvg下后门在25%阈值以上持续平均336轮。

Conclusion: 上下文触发的稀疏后门攻击具有低可检测性和长期持续性，对联邦监控系统构成严重实际威胁，需加强防御。

Abstract: Federated video anomaly detection trains model collaboratively without sharing raw surveillance footage, but limited server-side visibility lets compromised clients to inject backdoor via malicious updates. This paper introduces STAIN-FL, a stealthy targeted backdoor attack injection framework that uses naturally occurring surveillance conditions, including low-light scenes, indoor settings, and crowd density, as contextual triggers. STAIN-FL combines anomaly-to-benign label \textit{manipulation} with gradient masking over least-updated coordinates to preserve clean accuracy while inducing trigger-conditioned misclassification. We evaluate STAIN-FL on \texttt{UCF-Crime} using 1024-dimensional I3D features in a non-IID four-client multi-agency setting, comparing FedAvg and FedProx under sparse and continuous attacks. Results show that sparse attacks have low-detectability, operationally significant attacks rather than high-intensity attacks: they keep the mean clean-accuracy drop below $2\%$, yet still misclassify more than half of triggered anomalies at peak backdoor accuracy under FedAvg ($56.7\%$) and FedProx ($54.2\%$). Under FedAvg, the sparse backdoor remains above the $25\%$ backdoor-accuracy threshold for an average of $336$ post-attack rounds, highlighting the persistence risk of contextually triggered attacks in surveillance systems.

</details>


### [95] [NeuronGuard: Robust LLM Safety Alignment via Ablation-Aware Safety Signal Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23959)
*Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Yufei Xia,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: NeuronGuard是一种在微调阶段通过将安全信号重新分布到更广泛神经元上来同时防御越狱攻击和神经元级剪枝攻击的方法，在保持任务精度的同时将攻击成功率降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御仅针对单一攻击类型，而越狱攻击和神经元级攻击都利用安全信息集中在稀疏神经元子集这一共同弱点，因此需要一种同时应对两类攻击的统一防御。

Method: 在微调阶段动态识别安全关键神经元，通过周期刷新的逐层线性分类器定位它们，在故意神经元剪枝下强制拒绝行为，并用KL散度正则化保持分布一致性，同时使用随机梯度投影策略解决防御与任务目标之间的冲突。

Result: 在三个LLM、六种先进攻击策略及多模态设置下的实验显示，攻击成功率接近零，任务精度得以保持，并且对白盒自适应攻击同样有效。

Conclusion: NeuronGuard提供了一种可形式化保证降低ASR上限的鲁棒防御，展示了安全信号重分布是应对多样攻击的有效策略，且不影响模型下游实用性。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) remains brittle against a growing spectrum of attacks. Jailbreak attacks bypass safety mechanisms through crafted prompts, while neuron-level attacks directly prune safety-critical neurons post-deployment. Both exploit a common weakness: safety-relevant information concentrates in a sparse neuron subset. We present NeuronGuard, a fine-tuning-stage defense that simultaneously hardens LLMs against both attack classes by redistributing safety signals across a broader set of neurons. NeuronGuard dynamically identifies safety-critical neurons via periodically refreshed per-layer linear classifiers, forces refusal behavior under deliberate neuron ablation, and applies KL-divergence regularization for distributional consistency. A randomized gradient projection strategy preserves downstream task utility by resolving conflicts between the defense and task objectives. We provide a formal guarantee that NeuronGuard strictly reduces the attack success rate (ASR) upper bound, and experiments across three LLMs, six state-of-the-art attack strategies, and multimodal settings confirm near-zero ASR while maintaining task accuracy, including against white-box adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [96] [RAGSentinel: Certifiable Geometric Consensus for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23965)
*Yueyang Quan,Anjun Gao,Yufei Xia,Minghong Fang,Zhuqing Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出RAGSentinel，一种无需训练、无需标签的黑盒RAG防御方法，通过代理编码器度量查询条件化的隐藏状态偏移并过滤中毒文档，在多个数据集和攻击下显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG后检索防御依赖指令遵循、参数知识或文本一致性，均可被自适应攻击者模仿或优化，缺乏黑盒场景下的鲁棒防御。

Method: 利用代理编码器计算检索文档引起的查询条件化隐藏状态偏移，移除共享主题方向后，基于鲁棒多数共识将中毒文档作为几何离群点过滤。

Result: 在三个问答数据集、三个LLM家族和多种投毒攻击下，一致实现低攻击成功率，同时保持有竞争力的准确性，并对拥有完整管线知识的自适应攻击有效。

Conclusion: RAGSentinel在诚实多数假设和表征分离条件下可精确恢复无毒的多数大小上下文，为黑盒RAG系统提供了一种高效且鲁棒的防御方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in external documents, but it also exposes a critical security vulnerability: adversarial documents injected into the knowledge database can enter the context window and steer the model toward targeted incorrect answers. Existing post-retrieval defenses rely on instruction following, parametric knowledge, or text-level consistency, all of which can be imitated or optimized against by adaptive attackers. We propose RAGSentinel, a training-free, label-free defense for black-box RAG systems. RAGSentinel uses a surrogate encoder to measure query-conditioned hidden-state shifts induced by retrieved documents, removes shared topic directions, and filters poisoned documents as geometric outliers from a robust majority consensus. We prove that, under an honest-majority assumption and a representation-level separation condition, RAGSentinel exactly recovers a poison-free majority-sized context. Experiments across three question-answering datasets, three LLM families, and multiple poisoning attacks show that RAGSentinel consistently achieves low attack success rates while preserving competitive accuracy and remaining effective against adaptive attacks with full pipeline knowledge.

</details>


### [97] [WebMCP-Phalanx: Enforcing and Characterizing Trust Boundaries for Browser-Integrated LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24017)
*Lin-Fa Lee,YI-YU Chang,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出WebMCP-Phalanx双代理运行时架构，通过浏览器原生信任锚和隔离式LLM审查，解决WebMCP工具集成中的主体归属、生命周期和提示注入问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W3C WebMCP提案让LLM代理调用网页工具，但当前SOP安全模型无法为多参与者环境下的工具提供足够溯源与生命周期保障，导致主体归属欺骗、工具生命周期失控和语义提示注入三类风险。

Method: 双代理架构：第一层用密码学能力凭证绑定工具与注册主体并传播溯源标签；第二层用无工具调用权的隔离Q-LLM审查元数据和输出，验证后转发给有执行权的P-LLM，同时隐藏Q-LLM内部状态，并引入调用时机门控延迟工具执行。

Result: 浏览器原生所有权机制将撤销与覆盖攻击成功率从100%降至0%；双代理运行时拦截全部80次描述注入攻击，工具返回攻击仅2/80成功；任务效用与无攻击基线统计无差异，但白盒自适应攻击可通过恶意工具名绕过描述过滤。

Conclusion: 验证了双代理隔离与溯源机制的有效性，但暴露描述过滤的时序漏洞，需以调用时机门控补充，确保所有工具元数据验证后再执行调用。

Abstract: The emerging W3C WebMCP proposal enables LLM agents to invoke tools exposed by web pages. In multi-party web environments, however, integrating agent execution into a browser security model centered on the Same-Origin Policy (SOP) leaves insufficient provenance and lifecycle guarantees for agent-accessible tools, creating three risks: subject-attribution spoofing, uncontrolled tool lifecycles, and semantic prompt injection. We propose WebMCP-Phalanx, a dual-layer agent runtime architecture. Its first layer provides a browser-native trust anchor that binds each tool to its registering principal through cryptographically protected capability credentials and propagates provenance labels throughout the tool lifecycle. Its second layer separates semantic inspection from privileged tool use. A Quarantine Agent (Q-LLM), without tool invocation authority, inspects tool metadata, outputs, and page-supplied content for prompt injection. Validated content is then forwarded to a Privileged Agent (P-LLM) for execution, while the Q-LLM's internal state remains hidden from page scripts. Empirical evaluation shows that the browser-native ownership mechanism reduces revocation and overwrite attack success from 100\% to 0\%. The dual-agent runtime blocks all 80 prompt-injection attempts embedded in tool descriptions and limits tool-return attacks to 2 successful cases out of 80. Across experiments, task utility remains statistically indistinguishable from the no-attack baseline. Under a white-box adaptive attacker, however, description-based filtering can be bypassed through malicious tool names invoked before inspection. This finding motivates a call-timing gate that delays tool invocation until all agent-visible tool metadata has been validated.

</details>


### [98] [What Guides the Agent? Adjudicating Unauthorized Behavior via Localizing Behavior-Guiding Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24022)
*Yichao Gao,Yumo Zhang,Yunhao Yao,Haohua Du,Puhan Luo,Ruiqi Li,Zhiqiang Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Attnlocate，一种运行时框架，通过将上下文跨度定位转化为目标检测任务并分析注意力矩阵中的激活痕迹，实现对LLM代理中行为引导指令的细粒度定位与恶意调用判定，以防御注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要依赖输入/输出层的静态检测或隔离，无法捕捉模型推理过程中动态产生的恶意诱导，且统一自然语言上下文通道使外部数据易被解析为指令，存在安全漏洞。

Method: 将定位问题建模为目标检测：设计多头部、多层注意力聚合方案构建词级特征空间，采用带无锚点检测头的1-D U-Net检测行为引导跨度，并根据来源权威性动态裁决恶意调用。

Result: 在五个LLM家族的十种代理配置（覆盖间接提示注入和工具投毒）下，平均IoU为0.743，AUROC达0.956，真阳性率0.934（假阳性率0.067），并跨未见模型有效迁移，支持无需重训的权威策略调整。

Conclusion: Attnlocate提供了一种有效的运行时防御机制，可精准定位并拦截恶意指令，提升LLM代理安全性，且具备跨模型泛化能力与策略灵活性。

Abstract: LLM agents integrated with external resources gain complex task capabilities, yet the unified natural-language context channel makes them vulnerable to injection attacks: untrusted external data may be dynamically parsed as behavior-guiding instructions during LLM inference, thereby subverting the agent's decision. Existing defenses focus on static detection or isolation of malicious content at the input/output level, remains insufficient for detecting such dynamic inducements that arise during model reasoning. We propose Attnlocate, a runtime framework for fine-grained localization of context spans that genuinely influence tool-calling decisions, i.e., behavior-guiding instructions. Attnlocate casts this localization problem as an object detection task, aiming to detect the distinctive activation traces induced by behavior-guiding instructions within the attention matrix. Specifically, we design a multi-head, multi-layer attention aggregation scheme to construct a token-level feature space tailored for object detection. Then, a 1-D U-Net equipped with an anchor-free detection head is deployed to detect these spans. Finally, based on the authority of the provider from which the detected behavior-guiding spans originate, Attnlocate dynamically adjudicates malicious invocation attempts. We evaluate Attnlocate across ten agent configurations from five LLM families, covering scenarios involving indirect prompt injection and tool poisoning. Attnlocate achieves a mean IoU of 0.743, an average AUROC of 0.956, and a 0.934 true-positive rate at 0.067 false-positive rate. It also transfers effectively across unseen models and supports authority policy adaptation without retraining.

</details>


### [99] [Anatomy of a Scam Call: What 10,000 real scam and spam calls reveal about how phone scammers operate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24127)
*Ethan Traister,Ankit Raj,Jiaqi Gan,Xingyu Shen,Tyler Wu,Yuchen Zhou,Tommy Duong,Kidus Zewde,Siying Chen,Simiao Ren*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过对10,211通诈骗/垃圾电话的完整语料分析，发现电话诈骗呈模板化产业特征——工作时间规律、脚本重复、优先索取身份锚点；随机化实验表明目标年龄仅影响诈骗者投入的对话轮数，不影响其索取内容；早期检测基准显示开场白即可预测升级倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电话诈骗普遍且代价高昂，但其内部运作机制因缺乏大规模观测数据而鲜为人知，本研究旨在利用AI语音代理蜜罐收集的完整语料，揭示诈骗与垃圾电话的运作规律及目标身份对诈骗行为的影响。

Method: 使用AI语音代理蜜罐收集54天内10,211通呼入诈骗/垃圾电话（913小时音频、330,956轮对话），区分诈骗与合法但掠夺性的线索生成；对1,823通随机化呼叫注入十种虚构身份，分析目标年龄对对话轮数和敏感信息索取的影响；并以开场白为基准，对比词袋分类器与微调设备端语言模型的升级预测性能。

Result: 诈骗电话在工作时间上呈6.6倍工作日/周末差异；前五个开场聚类覆盖一半流量；身份锚点（家庭住址、出生日期）比支付凭证更常被索取。目标年龄每增加十年，诈骗者多投入约15%的对话轮数（率比1.15，p=0.005），但敏感信息索取率无显著变化（OR=0.99）。开场白第一句预测ROC-AUC为0.72，第八句达0.87，词袋分类器与微调语言模型表现相当。

Conclusion: 电话诈骗呈现出模板化产业特征：诈骗者根据目标年龄调整投入强度，但不改变索取内容；早期检测从开场白即可实现，简单模型即可达到与复杂模型相当的预测性能。

Abstract: Telephone fraud is pervasive and costly, but its inner workings are rarely observed at scale. We analyze a complete corpus of 10,211 inbound scam and spam calls -- 913 hours of audio and 330,956 transcribed turns from 5,780 distinct numbers -- collected over 54 days by an AI voice-agent honeypot that answered callers and kept them talking, and introduced in a companion data descriptor. We separate outright scams, which solicit sensitive information, from the larger stream of predatory but legal lead generation ("spam") that feeds them. Scam operations keep office hours (6.6x more calls per weekday than weekend day); thousands of disposable numbers run a small catalog of recycled scripts (thirty opening clusters, half the traffic in the top five); and callers solicit identity anchors -- a home address and a date of birth -- far more often than payment credentials, pressing through persistence and manufactured authority rather than overt threats. Our central experiment asks: does it matter who picks up? Every seeded lead carried one of ten fictitious identities drawn uniformly at random, so the identity a fraud operation reaches is fixed before the caller exists. Across 1,823 randomized calls, scammers spent about 15% more conversational turns per decade of the target's apparent age (rate ratio 1.15, 95% CI 1.08-1.23; randomization p = 0.005) -- yet what they asked for did not change (26.3% of calls reached a request for sensitive information; odds ratio 0.99 per decade, 95% CI 0.90-1.08). A second experiment casts early detection as a benchmark: from a scammer's opening lines alone, on a caller-disjoint split, escalation is predictable at 0.72 ROC-AUC from the first line and 0.87 by the eighth, and a plain bag-of-words classifier matches a fine-tuned on-device language model. Telephone fraud emerges as a templated industry that varies how hard it works a target, but not what it wants.

</details>


### [100] [Gated Activation Steering for Reducing Sycophancy & Hallucination in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23666)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Subash Neupane,Shaswata Mitra,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于推理时干预（ITI）的联合控制框架，通过分离的引导方向和门控机制，同时缓解大语言模型在临床问答中的幻觉与谄媚行为，且不干预每个对话轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如提示防护或持续激活引导）要么单独处理幻觉或谄媚，要么对所有轮次进行粗粒度干预，可能损害原本正确的回答。在临床场景中，模型需保持对上下文的忠实并抵抗用户施压，亟需一种能按需干预的统一框架。

Method: 从对比临床样本中学习幻觉和谄媚的独立引导方向，仅作用于经因果验证的注意力头；运行时通过行为专用门控判断是否触发干预，幻觉门抑制无依据表述，谄媚门阻止因用户压力导致的答案偏移，模型权重保持不变。

Result: 共进行15,900次模型响应测试。在600条压力轨迹中，未引导的4B模型有570次屈服，而门控引导帮助其在551次中坚持更久；其抗压能力与超100B参数模型相当。

Conclusion: 针对性的推理时引导可在不干预每轮的情况下显著提升模型在临床问答中的鲁棒性，为资源受限场景下的对齐提供有效路径。

Abstract: Sycophancy and hallucination are persistent failure modes of Large Language Models (LLMs) across domains. However, it becomes particularly consequential in clinical question answering, where responses must remain grounded in the provided context and robust to user pressure. Hallucination can introduce information that is unsupported by the context, while sycophancy can cause a model to abandon a previously correct answer when challenged by the user. Existing approaches, such as prompt-based safeguards and always-on activation steering, often address these behaviors separately or apply interventions broadly across turns, which can unnecessarily deteriorate responses that were already correct. To address these limitations within a single framework, we employ Inference Time Intervention (ITI) to jointly control both behaviors by learning separate steering directions for hallucination and sycophancy from contrastive clinical pairs and applying them to causally verified attention heads. During runtime, behavior-specific gates then determine when intervention is needed: the hallucination component mitigates unsupported claims, while the sycophancy component mitigates answer shifts caused by user pressure. We evaluate this framework on clinical questions grounded in EHR data while keeping the model weights frozen. Across all evaluation settings, we conducted 15,900 model-response runs. Across 600 pressure trajectories for the 4-billion-parameter model, the unsteered model caved in 570 cases. At the same time, gated steering helped it last longer in 551 of them. It held its ground under pressure at levels comparable to those of models with more than 100 billion parameters, showing that targeted inference-time steering can improve robustness without intervening at every turn.

</details>


### [101] [Not All Tokens Are Equal: Region-Aware Consistency Repair of Backdoors in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24354)
*Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Mingkun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,Jun Sun,Guoheng Sun,Xiaoning Ren,Haijun Wang,Ting Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出RACER框架，通过检测和归一化多模态大模型中由后门引起的层间不一致性异常，仅用100个干净样本即可在模型层面消除后门，无需触发器知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门移除方法对MLLM效果有限，推理时防御不消除模型内嵌后门，缺乏源头修复手段。

Method: 分解融合表示为视觉和文本token区域，分别归一化层间不一致性，用模态感知权重重组，构建区域感知不一致目标，通过min-max优化生成最坏扰动并对抗微调。

Result: 在3个开源MLLM、36种后门设置下，平均ASR降至1.1%，32种设置下为0%，且保持干净任务性能。

Conclusion: RACER能有效且高效地消除MLLM中的多模态后门，不依赖触发器知识，适用于干净和带后门模型。

Abstract: MLLMs are increasingly deployed in user-facing applications, yet they inherit backdoor risks from the pipelines used to construct them: triggers may reside in images, texts, or both. Existing model-level backdoor removal methods, largely designed for conventional classifiers, show limited effectiveness on MLLMs, while MLLM-specific defenses mainly operate at inference time, filtering suspicious inputs without removing the backdoor embedded in the model. To address this gap and eliminate latent backdoors from MLLMs at their source, we present RACER, a model-level repair framework motivated by a key observation: backdoors induce abnormal layer-to-layer evolution in internal representations, which we term the layer-wise inconsistency anomaly. Importantly, this anomaly is modality-dependent, concentrating primarily in the token region encoding the trigger features that the backdoor model actually relies on. RACER therefore decomposes the fused representation into visual and textual token regions, normalizes their layer-wise inconsistency separately, and recomposes them using modality-aware weights over a deep-layer window, yielding a region-aware inconsistency objective that better captures localized backdoor-induced anomalies. Through a min-max optimization, this objective drives worst-case perturbation synthesis and adversarial fine-tuning against the resulting perturbation to repair the model, suppressing the deep representational directional shifts on which backdoor behaviors rely. RACER requires only 100 clean samples and no knowledge of the trigger, attack objective, or even whether the input model contains a backdoor. Evaluations on three open-source MLLMs across 36 backdoor settings spanning image, text, and multimodal triggers show that RACER reduces the average ASR to 1.1%, reaching 0% in 32 settings, while preserving clean-task utility on both backdoor and clean models.

</details>


### [102] [Automata from Agent Traces: Failure and Next-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23670)
*Seonglae Cho,Franklin Cardenoso Fernandez,Umar Mohammed,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 将LLM智能体的完整轨迹语料库压缩为单个紧凑的有限状态机（FSM），以捕获跨运行的行为拓扑，同时支持下一步预测和失败预测，并在12个公开数据集上验证了其紧凑性、高保真度及有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法按轨迹或仅基于成功案例分析，忽略了跨运行的拓扑结构，导致长轨迹难以审计和监控。

Method: 将整个轨迹语料库折叠为一个紧凑的FSM作为结构基底，提取状态上下文用于下一步预测，并利用每状态行为特征进行失败预测及在线监控。

Result: FSM仅7-43个状态，重放保真度≥0.997，构建耗时毫秒级；下一步预测优于Agent Workflow Memory，失败预测AUROC达0.94，并能从部分轨迹实现早停。

Conclusion: 行为拓扑主要由部署环境而非LLM本身决定，FSM可作为与模型无关的结构化原始工具，用于安全审计和运行时监控。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-step tasks, but their behavioral structure remains opaque: long unstructured traces resist the safety auditing and runtime monitoring that deployment requires. Existing approaches operate per-trace or success-only, so they miss the cross-run topology that links next-step and failure prediction. To recover that shared structure, we collapse an entire trace corpus into a single, compact finite-state machine (FSM) that serves as a structural substrate for the otherwise unpredictable behavior of LLM agents. Across twelve public datasets, the FSMs are compact (7-43 states), replay held-out data at >=0.997 fitness with near-identical topology across splits, and build in milliseconds. This substrate addresses both prediction goals. For next-step prediction, FSM-state context outperforms Agent Workflow Memory on every ground-truth-matched dataset. For failure prediction, per-state behavioral features reach held-out AUROC up to 0.94, and an online monitor ranks failing runs above passing ones from a partial trace, triggering early stopping well before completion. Behavioral topology thus appears shaped more by the deployment harness than by the LLM, providing a model-agnostic structural primitive for safety auditing and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [103] [A Drop-in KEM Replacement for Client Signatures in Post-Quantum SSH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24447)
*Hongbo Liu,Yufan Su,Jiangxia Ge,Qionglu Zhang,Zhaoxuan Li,Xianhui Lu,Li Song,Wenhua Gao,Li Zhou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于KEM的SSH用户认证方法，替代公钥签名，在保持凭据模型的同时降低延迟和服务器计算开销，并通过OpenSSH实现和实验验证其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子签名在SSH认证中带来大小和计算开销，导致延迟、流量和服务器负载增加；KEM认证可作为自然替代，尤其在已受保护且可扩展的SSH用户认证层。

Method: 设计一种会话绑定的挑战-响应KEM认证方案，融入现有SSH用户认证框架，保留公钥凭据模型，支持增量部署；基于后量子ACCE框架提供安全性证明，并在OpenSSH中用liboqs实现。

Result: 在代表性网络条件下，KEM认证与紧凑签名认证竞争力相当；相比大签名混合基线，中位握手延迟降低约10%；小TCP初始窗口下延迟比ML-DSA低7.3%、比SLH-DSA低17.9%，服务器端在线加密成本比同类别ML-DSA低59.1%。

Conclusion: KEM认证是后量子SSH中签名替换的有效替代方案，在传输或计算受限场景下优势明显，且易于增量部署，为后量子迁移提供更优路径。

Abstract: The transition to post-quantum cryptography is reshaping the Secure Shell (SSH) protocol for remote administration. Post-quantum key exchange has been deployed in OpenSSH and is being standardized, while SSH authentication largely remains a signature-replacement effort. This path preserves the familiar public-key credential model, but inherits the size and computation overhead of post-quantum signatures, which can increase latency, traffic, and server-side load. KEM-based authentication offers a natural alternative to this signature-centric path, and SSH makes this especially attractive at the user-authentication layer, which is method-extensible, separated from transport-layer key exchange and host-key authentication, and already protected by the established channel.
  We present a drop-in KEM-based user-authentication method for SSH that replaces client public-key signatures with a session-bound challenge-response proof. The method fits into SSH's existing user-authentication framework, preserving the public-key credential model and enabling incremental deployment alongside existing methods. We provide a reduction-based security argument in the post-quantum ACCE framework, implement the design in OpenSSH using liboqs, and evaluate it under representative RTTs, TCP initial-window settings, and post-quantum migration configurations. Our results show that KEM-based authentication is competitive with compact signature-based authentication under representative network settings, while reducing median handshake latency by up to about 10% against large-signature hybrid baselines. The advantages are clearer when post-quantum signatures stress transmission or computation: median latency under small TCP initial windows falls by up to 7.3% versus ML-DSA and 17.9% versus SLH-DSA, while server-side online cryptographic cost is 59.1% lower than that for ML-DSA in the same NIST category.

</details>


### [104] [Defending Network Intrusion Detection Systems Based on Graph Neural Networks Against Structural Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24454)
*Dimitri Galli,Andrea Venturi,Dario Stabili,Mauro Andreolini,Mirco Marchetti*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出基于对抗训练的防御框架，通过替换正常网络流中的源/目的节点生成对抗样本，增强GNN-NIDS对结构攻击的鲁棒性。在CTU-13和TON-IoT数据集上验证，E-GraphSAGE模型在干净图上的检测性能和对抗鲁棒性均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在NIDS中表现优秀，但对结构对抗攻击（如边注入、节点插入）脆弱，现有防御方法依赖不切实际的假设，需提出实用防御方案。

Method: 对抗训练：通过替换良性流中的源/目的节点模拟边注入攻击，生成对抗样本并加入训练，强化E-GraphSAGE基分类器。

Result: 在CTU-13和TON-IoT数据集上，硬化后检测器在干净图上的检测性能更优，且对结构对抗攻击的鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: 所提对抗训练框架有效提升GNN-NIDS的结构攻击鲁棒性，同时不牺牲干净数据性能，为实际网络安全场景提供可行防御方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) represent a promising solution for Machine Learning (ML) based Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDS), thanks to their ability to leverage both network flow features and topological patterns. While GNN classifiers demonstrate superior robustness against feature-based adversarial attacks compared to other ML detectors, they remain vulnerable to structural adversarial attacks, where an attacker perturbs the underlying network graph topology by injecting edges or inserting nodes. Such attacks pose a realistic and severe threat, undermining the reliability of GNN-based NIDS in practical deployments. While countermeasures have been proposed in the literature, they often rely on assumptions that are unrealistic in real-world cybersecurity scenarios. In this paper, we propose a defense framework based on adversarial training to strengthen GNN-based NIDS against structural attacks. We generate adversarial samples by strategically replacing the source and destination nodes in benign network flows, thereby efficiently mimicking edge injection attacks. We evaluate our approach on two widely used datasets (CTU-13 and TON-IoT) using E-GraphSAGE as the base GNN classifier. Experimental results show that our approach produces hardened detectors with superior detection performance on clean graphs and enhanced robustness against structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [105] [AgentRoom: Concurrent Multi-Agent Coding in a CRDT-Backed Shared Workspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23740)
*Seonglae Cho,Donghyun Lee*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: AgentRoom 是一个为并发编码智能体设计的实时协作编辑协议，通过 CRDT 合并共享文件系统上的文件级声明、状态和广播工具，使多个智能体能够并行协作。实验表明，在匹配计算资源下，AgentRoom 相比串行或并行合并基线能减少任务放弃率，且协调机制（而非并行性或 CRDT 合并）是性能提升的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并发多智能体编码系统受限于 LLM 逐 token 生成的串行瓶颈：要么通过阶段交接串行化智能体，要么无协调地池化独立样本。此外，单智能体在困难任务上常以单文件占位实现并放弃（放弃率高达 50%）。需要一种能支持真正并发协作的协议，借鉴人类团队实时协作编辑的方案（CRDT），解决多智能体协调问题。

Method: AgentRoom 构建于 CRDT 合并的共享文件系统之上，运行时层将文件级声明（claim）、状态（status）和广播（broadcast）暴露为 MCP 工具。五个前沿编码 CLI 模型在四个后端任务上运行，包括 Python DevBench 和 Rust+axum 的跨语言测试。通过对比 Solo、并行合并（parallel-merge）以及部分消融（如无协调、无 CRDT 合并）来评估性能。

Result: 对于 CLI 稳定的模型，使用 2 个智能体的 AgentRoom 比 Solo 放弃的任务更少，且运行间变异更小。在匹配计算资源下，一个正的平均 LLM 评判对比显示 AgentRoom 优于并行合并；另一个对比（bundle probe）显示完整 AgentRoom 优于每个部分消融案例，且这种优势是排序性的（不是百分比分割）。

Conclusion: AgentRoom 的并发协作协议能有效减少任务放弃率并提升稳定性，其核心增益来源于协调机制（coordination），而非并行性或 CRDT 合并本身。这为多智能体编码系统的设计提供了新方向：应优先优化智能体间的协调协议，而非仅依赖并行采样或数据同步技术。

Abstract: Concurrent multi-agent coding promises division of labor across modules, robustness through redundancy, and parallel exploration at the natural granularity of multi-file projects. Realtime collaborative editing protocols solve this coordination problem for human teams via Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs), but the LLMs underneath generate one token at a time and existing multi-agent coding systems inherit this serial limit: they either sequence agents through phase handoffs or pool independent samples without coordination, and a single agent abandons up to half of hard tasks with a one-file stub-and-exit. AgentRoom is a realtime collaborative editing protocol for concurrent coding agents. Its runtime layer exposes file-level claim, status, and broadcast as MCP tools on a CRDT-merged shared filesystem. Five frontier coding-CLI models ran four backend coding tasks, with cross-language checks in Python DevBench and Rust+axum. For CLI-stable models, AgentRoom with 2 agents abandons fewer tasks than Solo and has less run-to-run variation. At matched-compute, one positive mean LLM-judge contrast puts AgentRoom over parallel-merge. The other contrast, a bundle probe, puts full AgentRoom above each partial case: an ordering rather than a percentage split. Coordination, not parallelism or CRDT-merge, bears the load.

</details>


### [106] [SeriCrypt: An LLM-Driven Context-Aware Serialization Framework for Cryptographic Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24498)
*Maosong Chen,Xi Chen,Mengcheng Ju,Dongliang Zhao,Chunxiang Gu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出 SeriCrypt，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的上下文感知序列化框架，用于从非结构化协议规范中自动提取约束并生成语法正确且密码学有效的消息序列，支持协议状态机学习、一致性测试和模糊测试，并在 TLS 1.2/1.3、IKEv1/v2、SSH 和 TLCP 上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动方法主要针对纯文本或明文协议，密码协议的消息构造仍依赖人工，因为其涉及跨消息状态依赖和密码学计算约束，缺乏自动化支持。

Method: 使用 LLM 从非结构化协议规范中提取字段约束、状态依赖和密码学计算规则，统一转换为领域特定语言（CDSL）的结构化中间表示，并由协议无关的执行引擎解析 CDSL 声明，自动化完成字段值解析、密码学原语调用和字节流序列化。

Result: 在 TLS 1.2/1.3、IKEv1/v2、SSH 和 TLCP 的主流实现上生成的消息序列全部被接受，所有场景完成握手；安全约束测试发现 5 个规范违反，模糊测试在相同时间预算下达到更深的协议状态和更高的代码覆盖率。

Conclusion: SeriCrypt 证明了 LLM 驱动的上下文感知序列化在密码协议安全测试中的实用价值，可自动化消息构造并有效支持一致性测试和模糊测试。

Abstract: Constructing syntactically correct and cryptographically valid message sequences is essential for protocol state machine learning, conformance testing, and fuzzing. Unlike plaintext protocols, cryptographic protocols involve complex cross-message state dependencies and cryptographic computation constraints. Existing automated approaches predominantly target text-based or plaintext protocols, leaving cryptographic message construction largely manual. We present SeriCrypt, an LLM-driven, context-aware serialization framework for cryptographic protocols. It employs a large language model to extract field constraints, state dependencies, and cryptographic computation rules from unstructured protocol specifications into a unified structured intermediate representation, formally characterized by a domain-specific language for cryptographic protocols (CDSL). A protocol-agnostic execution engine parses CDSL declarations, automating field value resolution, cryptographic primitive invocation, and byte-stream serialization. As case studies in protocol security testing, we use the framework to construct violation messages targeting specification-defined security constraints and to support protocol fuzzing, evaluating it on mainstream implementations of TLS 1.2/1.3, IKEv1/v2, SSH, and TLCP. SeriCrypt generated message sequences accepted by all evaluated implementations and completed handshakes in every scenario. Security constraint testing revealed five specification violations, and fuzzing reached deeper protocol states with higher code coverage than mainstream fuzzers under the same time budget, demonstrating the framework's practical value for cryptographic protocol security testing.

</details>


### [107] [Who Falls for SMiSh? Learning Through Survey Data Where to Best Target Awareness Training for Mobile Messaging Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24669)
*Cori Faklaris,Sarah Tabassum,Heather Richter Lipford*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过两项大规模调查，发现年轻人和大学生对SMiShing攻击尤为脆弱，且难以识别合法短信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动诈骗激增，但缺乏关于谁最易受SMiShing攻击的数据，以往研究多关注电子邮件钓鱼。

Method: 使用已发表的模拟方法，对美国成年手机用户代表性样本进行两项大规模调查。

Result: 年轻个体和大学生特别脆弱，参与者在识别合法信息上表现困难，第二项研究比较了金融信息变体。

Conclusion: 研究者、监管机构和电信商应针对24岁以下及大学生用户制定移动专属干预措施，并增加验证和警告。

Abstract: As mobile phone adoption has surged, so have scams involving these devices. One such scam, known as SMiShing (or smishing) after Short Message Service (SMS), involves fraudsters sending phishing links via mobile texts. Despite the prevalence of SMiShing, there is a lack of data on who is most vulnerable to these attacks. Prior research on phishing (its email counterpart) suggests that susceptibility may vary by demographic and contextual factors. In two large-scale surveys, we use a previously published simulation method to collect data from representative samples of U.S. adult mobile phone users. Our findings indicate that younger individuals and college students are particularly vulnerable. Participants struggled to correctly identify legitimate messages, with the second study providing comparisons of financial message variants. Researchers, regulators, and telecoms can help users by creating mobile-specific interventions for under-24 and university customers and adding verifications and warnings.

</details>


### [108] [ICS Cybersecurity Datasets: A Systematic Meta-Review of Coverage, Evaluation Practice, and Structural Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24757)
*Konstantinos E. Kampourakis,Vyron Kampourakis,Georgios Kambourakis,Sokratis Katsikas,Stefanos Gritzalis,Mario Rodríguez-Béjar,José Luis Hernández-Ramos*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 对2019至2026年间18项研究进行元审查，梳理83个ICS安全数据集，提出五维统一分类法，揭示数据集的系统性结构偏差（如战术阶段集中、缺少L0现场设备数据、运营数据占比低等），并指出评估实践存在流式评估缺失、划分不严格、可复现性差等问题，最终提出三类结构性失衡及协调研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICS入侵检测研究高度依赖公开数据集，但缺乏对整体数据集语料库是否支持当前评估论断的系统性评估；即首次对数据集的集体结构、评估实践及其耦合关系进行元层面审查，以填补空白。

Method: 采用元审查方法，从18项研究（2019—2026）中识别、整理并统一表征83个ICS相关数据集，使用统一五维分类法进行结构化分析；同时对评估实践进行并行审计，并开展分类法与评估实践之间的耦合分析。

Result: 数据集呈结构性偏斜：85.5%集中于晚阶段OT干扰战术，仅8.4%含跨阶段IT/OT推进序列，Purdue第0级现场设备数据基本缺失，运营来源数据仅占15.7%；评估上无研究报告流式评估，不足半数采用规范训练/测试划分，仅2项满足可复现要求；耦合分析显示数据集失衡限制了若干评估实践的可行性和范围。

Conclusion: 识别出三大结构性失衡：架构浅层性、进程压缩和跨域替代；据此提出协调的研究议程，包括跨阶段语料构建、时间结构化基准测试、事件级标签标准及运营数据共享治理框架。

Abstract: Intrusion detection research in Industrial Control Systems (ICS) heavily depends on public datasets, yet no prior work has systematically assessed whether the collective dataset corpus supports current evaluation claims. This paper addresses this gap through a meta-review of 18 studies between 2019 and 2026, from which 83 ICS, or ICS directly related, cybersecurity datasets are identified, harmonised, and characterised using a unified five-dimensional taxonomy. The taxonomy reveals that the corpus is structurally skewed: 85.5% of datasets concentrate on late-stage OT Disruption tactics, cross-stage IT/OT progression sequences are present in only 8.4% of cases, field-device evidence at Level 0 of the Purdue hierarchy is effectively absent, and operationally sourced data accounts for only 15.7% of the collection. A parallel audit of evaluation practices shows that zero report streaming evaluation, fewer than half apply disciplined train/test partitioning, and only two satisfy reproducibility requirements. Furthermore, a taxonomy-evaluation coupling analysis shows that dataset imbalances constrain the scope and feasibility of several evaluation practices. Based on these findings, we identify three structural imbalances: i) architectural shallowness, ii) progression compression, and iii) cross-domain substitution, and derive a coordinated research agenda which covers cross-stage corpus construction, temporally structured benchmarking, event-level label standards, and governance frameworks for operational data sharing.

</details>


### [109] [A Formal Methodological Framework for Auditing Robustness and Fidelity in Explainable AI: From Application to Trust Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23817)
*Rosa Elysabeth Ralinirina,Jean Christian Ralaivao,Niaiko Michaël Ralaivao,Alain Josué Ratovondrahona,Thomas Mahatody*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种审计协议，通过鲁棒性和保真度两个指标计算信任分数，用于评估事后解释器（如SHAP和LIME）在输入扰动下的可靠性。在马达加斯加数据集上的实验表明，即使模型AUC很高，解释仍可能退化或无信息，强调审计XAI输出的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHAP和LIME等解释工具在输入受噪声扰动时输出可能大幅变化，这在粮食安全研究中被观察到，引发了对解释可信度的质疑。现有评估方法未系统量化这种不稳定性和解释与模型实际行为的一致性。

Method: 构建审计协议，测量事后解释器的两个属性：(1) 鲁棒性——解释在输入扰动下的稳定性；(2) 保真度——重要特征是否真正驱动模型预测。将两者合并为单一信任分数。实验使用马达加斯加多部门数据集（83特征、253记录、4类营养不良），采用3种分类器、2种解释器及正则化版本进行评估。

Result: 模型AUC超过0.99时，解释仍可能产生数值退化或完全无信息；当模型过拟合时，保真度分数失去判别能力。正则化版本未完全解决这些问题。

Conclusion: 审计XAI输出是必要的而非可选的，尤其在敏感领域（如粮食安全）中用于决策时，不能仅依赖高模型性能或解释器默认输出。

Abstract: SHAP and LIME are now standard tools for interpreting black-box predictions, yet their outputs can vary substantially when the input is perturbed by small amounts of noise--a problem we observed firsthand in our previous work on food security in Madagascar (Ralinirina et al., 2025). This variability raises the question of whether such explanations can be trusted at all. We address it by constructing an auditing protocol that measures two properties of any post-hoc explainer: robustness (how stable the explanation is under input perturbation) and fidelity (whether the features deemed important actually drive the model's prediction). These two quantities are combined into a single Trust Score. We run the protocol on a multi-sectoral dataset from Madagascar (83 features, 253 records, 4 malnutrition classes) using three classifiers and two explainers, plus their regularized counterparts. The results are sobering: models with AUC above 0.99 can produce numerically degenerate or flatly uninformative explanations, and fidelity scores lose discriminative power when the model is overfitted. These findings suggest that auditing XAI outputs is not optional but necessary, particularly when they inform decisions in sensitive domains.

</details>


### [110] [Research Methodologies for Cybersecurity in Enterprise Environments: A Narrative Review, Synthesis and Executable Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24850)
*Tran Duc Le*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文通过151篇文献的综述和11个方法论家族的可执行协议，论证了企业网络安全研究应采用方法论多元主义，并强调以明确的效度推理来匹配研究设计与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业网络安全研究涉及方法众多，研究者面临方法选择问题，且文献中结果矛盾（如入侵检测算法排名不一致），需要系统性方法论指导。

Method: 基于151篇文献的叙事综述，归纳出11个方法论家族，每个家族转化为包含步骤、工具、评价标准、效度威胁和报告清单的可执行协议，并用视觉映射呈现流程与决策。

Result: 发现文献矛盾源于评价设计差异而非算法本身；提供了方法论家族的分类和协议，支持方法论多元主义与显式效度推理。

Conclusion: 研究者须将评价设计与研究决策匹配，三角验证技术与组织证据，明确概括总体，并报告结果不成立的条件。

Abstract: Enterprise cybersecurity research draws on a wider range of methods than any single community routinely teaches. Researchers face a selection problem before they face a technical one: a study may simultaneously need a systematic review, a design-science artifact, a controlled detection experiment, an interview study, or an attack-graph model. This paper addresses that problem in two ways. First, it provides a narrative review and synthesis of methodological practices across a verified corpus of 151 works. We organise these practices into eleven methodology families, detailing for each what questions it answers, the strength of its supporting evidence, and its common failure modes. Second, we convert each family into an executable protocol comprising ordered steps, required instruments, evaluation criteria, common validity threats, and a reporting checklist. Every protocol is also visually mapped to make the sequence, decisions, and threats legible at a glance. We also treat contradictions in the literature as evidence. For example, reported rankings of intrusion-detection algorithms are wildly inconsistent across individually careful studies. We argue this pattern is most parsimoniously explained by variations in evaluation design rather than the algorithms themselves, as these studies differ in design dimensions known to shift results by more than the margins separating the algorithms. Ultimately, the evidence supports methodological pluralism disciplined by explicit validity reasoning. We conclude that researchers must match their evaluation design to the decision under study, triangulate technical against organisational evidence, explicitly state the population a result generalises to, and report the conditions under which the result would not hold.

</details>


### [111] [Prompt Structure Redistributes, Not Reduces: An Empirical Analysis of Security-Weaknesses in LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24857)
*Maitreyee Das Urmi,Jessica Pourleyli,Fabio Santos,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 结构化提示能大幅提升LLM代码生成的合规性（如GPT-4o无效输出从338降至37-52），但安全导向的提示并未一致降低漏洞总体发生率，主要将风险从高危向低危转移，并可能引发语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注安全提示是否消除漏洞，忽略了提示对生成代码的合规性、漏洞严重性及类型分布的影响，难以全面评估提示工程在LLM辅助开发中的安全效果。

Method: 使用424个安全敏感Python任务，对GPT-4o和LLaMA 3.1-8B施加五种逐步增强结构与安全引导的提示变体，利用Bandit和CodeQL评估生成合规性、漏洞普遍性、严重性及CWE分布。

Result: 结构化提示显著降低无效输出；安全提示未稳定减少漏洞总数，但GPT-4o高危漏洞占比从20.8%降至13.6%，低危占比从32%升至43.5%，LLaMA变化较弱且不一致；安全提示导致语义漂移，移除或改写安全敏感代码。

Conclusion: 提示结构提升合规性，但安全提示无法替代稳健的安全控制，需在LLM辅助开发中结合其他保障措施。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly generate code from natural-language prompts, making prompt engineering a key mechanism for shaping the security of generated software. Structured and security-oriented prompts are widely used to encourage safer code, yet their effects extend beyond whether detected weaknesses are simply present or absent. Using 424 security-sensitive Python tasks, we generate solutions with GPT-4o and LLaMA 3.1-8B under five prompt variants that progressively add structural and security guidance, and evaluate them with Bandit and CodeQL along two axes: generation compliance and security weakness prevalence, severity, and CWE distributions. Structured prompting substantially reduces refusals (e.g., GPT-4o invalid outputs drop from 338 of 424 to 37-52), enabling large-scale analysis, but security-oriented refinements do not consistently reduce overall weakness prevalence. For GPT-4o, stronger prompts primarily redistribute risk: high-severity findings fall (20.8% to 13.6%) while low-severity findings rise (32% to 43.5%); LLaMA shows weaker, less consistent shifts. We also observe security-driven semantic drift, where stricter prompts silently remove or rewrite explicitly requested unsafe constructs. Overall, prompt structure improves compliance but is an unreliable substitute for robust security controls in LLM-assisted development.

</details>


### [112] [SyPS: Measuring Sycophancy Prompt Sensitivity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23837)
*Lijia Huang,Yao Fu,Sihao Ren*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SyPS框架，评估大语言模型在保持语义不变、仅改变社交线索时谄媚行为的变化程度，并引入SPSS指标区分基线谄媚与提示诱导的偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谄媚评估仅在固定提示下测量，忽略了不同社交线索（如用户信心、情绪、社会共识、寻求认可）可能引发模型谄媚行为的不稳定性。

Method: 构建受控提示变体（SyPS），保留底层用户情境不变，仅改变谄媚相关的社交线索，并设计SPSS作为实例级指标，分离基线谄媚与提示诱导的谄媚变化。

Result: 谄媚提示敏感性具有社会结构性：寻求认可和情绪压力线索增加谄媚，而反向框架和反谄媚提示降低谄媚。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了LLM是否在保持稳定社会判断的同时，能恰当调整语气，并为模型鲁棒性比较提供新维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to exhibit social sycophancy, often validating or agreeing with users in socially sensitive contexts. Existing evaluations typically measure sycophancy under a fixed prompt formulation, leaving unclear whether such behavior is stable when the same underlying situation is presented with different sycophancy-relevant prompt variants. In this work, we study sycophancy prompt sensitivity: the extent to which changes in user confidence, emotional framing, social consensus, or validation-seeking language alter a model's sycophantic behavior. We refer to our evaluation framework as SyPS, short for Sycophancy Prompt Sensitivity. Building on existing social sycophancy evaluation settings, SyPS constructs controlled prompt variants that preserve the same underlying user situation while varying sycophancy-relevant social cues. We introduce the Sycophancy Prompt Sensitivity Score (SPSS), an instance-level measure of sycophancy variation across paired prompt variants.
